support). Customers will message a company's Twilio WhatsApp number. The `apps/server` will receive the webhook and 
save it. Support agents will reply from the Next.js frontend, which hits `apps/server`, which then delegates the 
actual external API call to Twilio via a BullMQ queue handled by `apps/worker`.\n\nPlease implement this 
step-by-step:\n\n---\n\n### STEP 1: Prisma Schema Updates (`packages/database/prisma/schema.prisma`)\n1. Add the 
following new Enums and Models to the bottom of the schema:\n\n```prisma\nenum IntegrationType {\n  twilio\n  
email_parse\n}\n\nmodel Integration {\n  id        Int             @id @default(autoincrement())\n  companyId Int\n  
provider  IntegrationType\n  identity  String          @unique @db.VarChar(255) // The Twilio WhatsApp number\n  
config    Json?\n\n  company   Company         @relation(fields: [companyId], references: [id])\n  
@@map(\"integrations\")\n}\n\nmodel Ticket {\n  id          Int       @id @default(autoincrement())\n  companyId   
Int\n  customerNum String    @db.VarChar(50) // Customer's WA number\n  status      String    @default(\"open\") // 
open, resolved\n  createdAt   DateTime  @default(now())\n  updatedAt   DateTime  @updatedAt\n\n  company     Company   
@relation(fields: [companyId], references: [id])\n  messages    Message[]\n\n  @@index([companyId, status])\n  
@@map(\"tickets\")\n}\n\nenum MessageDirection {\n  inbound\n  outbound\n}\n\nenum MessageChannel {\n  whatsapp\n  
sms\n  email\n}\n\nmodel Message {\n  id        Int              @id @default(autoincrement())\n  companyId Int\n  
ticketId  Int?\n  leadId    Int?\n  direction MessageDirection\n  channel   MessageChannel\n  \n  sender    String     
      @db.VarChar(255)\n  recipient String           @db.VarChar(255)\n  body      String           @db.Text\n  
createdAt DateTime         @default(now())\n\n  company   Company          @relation(fields: [companyId], references: 
[id])\n  ticket    Ticket?          @relation(fields: [ticketId], references: [id])\n  lead      Lead?            
@relation(fields: [leadId], references: [id])\n\n  @@index([companyId])\n  @@index([ticketId])\n  
@@map(\"messages\")\n}\n\n2.  CRITICAL: You must update the existing Company and Lead models in the schema\n    to 
include the reverse relations to prevent Prisma compilation errors:\n\n  - In Company, add: integrations Integration[] 
tickets Ticket[] messages\n    Message[]\n  - In Lead, add: messages Message[]\n\nSTEP 2: Main API Server - Inbound 
Webhook (apps/server)\n\nCreate apps/server/src/controllers/webhookController.ts:\n\n  - Build 
handleIncomingWhatsApp(req, res) to process public Twilio webhooks (Do\n    NOT apply protect middleware).\n  - 
Extract From, To, and Body from req.body.\n  - Find the Integration where identity === To to identify the companyId.\n 
 - Find an 'open' Ticket for this companyId and From number. If none exists,\n    create a new Ticket.\n  - Save the 
incoming message to the Message table linked to the Ticket.\n  - Return res.status(200).send('<Response></Response>') 
(Twilio TwiML\n    standard).\n\nSTEP 3: Main API Server - Outbound Controller (apps/server)\n\nCreate 
apps/server/src/controllers/supportController.ts:\n\n  - Build replyToTicket(req, res) (Protected by protect 
middleware).\n  - Find the Ticket by req.params.ticketId. Ensure it belongs to\n    req.user.companyId.\n  - Save the 
outbound message to the Message table immediately so it reflects in\n    the UI.\n  - Push a job to a BullMQ queue: 
whatsappQueue.add('send-reply', { ticketId,\n    to: customerNum, body }).\n  - Return 200 OK.\n\nSTEP 4: Worker 
Server Processing (apps/worker)\n\nCreate apps/worker/src/workers/whatsappWorker.ts:\n\n  - Initialize a BullMQ Worker 
listening to the queue used in Step 3.\n  - In the processor function, use the twilio SDK to send the message:\n    
twilioClient.messages.create({ from: 'whatsapp:COMPANY_NUMBER', to:\n    'whatsapp:CUSTOMER_NUMBER', body })\n  - 
Ensure errors are thrown so BullMQ automatically handles retries.\n\nSTEP 5: Frontend Support UI 
(apps/web/app/support/page.tsx)\n\nCreate a Next.js \"use client\" interface for the Support Agents:\n\n  - Layout: A 
two-pane chat interface (like WhatsApp Web).\n  - Left Pane: A list of active Tickets fetched from the API.\n  - Right 
Pane: The chat history (Messages) for the selected ticket.\n  - Input Area: A text box and \"Send\" button that calls 
the replyToTicket\n    endpoint.\n  - Style: Use Tailwind CSS or clean standard styles matching a modern chat 
UI.\n\nPlease provide the TypeScript code for all 5 steps, ensuring you import prisma\nfrom @repo/db across all
